if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
window.addEventListener('load', function () {
// Safety net: unregister any leftover worker from the old extension
// (different scriptURL at this scope) before registering the new one.
// Harmless if none exist.
navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations().then(function (regs) {
regs.forEach(function (reg) {
var activeUrl = reg.active ? reg.active.scriptURL : '';
if (activeUrl !== location.origin + '/sw.js') {
reg.unregister();
}
});
});
navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { scope: '/' })
.then(function (reg) {
console.log('[PWA] Service worker registered, scope:', reg.scope);
})
.catch(function (err) {
console.error('[PWA] Service worker registration failed:', err);
});
});
